- **spec**: Align on the idea before building. - **spec**: Derive a spec the unattended loop can run on. Enumerate the calls the current draft does not answer, then close each one by disposition: found in code (cite the path), a tuning value (set a default and move on), or a genuine guess (ask the human). The exit condition is not a rubric pass but zero unresolved, because a writer told to "fill in the gaps" invents answers and the next check waves them through, converging on a hallucination while every gate stays green. Use before /build when nobody has decided what done means, or when a draft reads complete but the loop would still have to guess.
